205	        if (rr < midX) rr = midX;
206	        if (t > midY) t = midY;
207	        if (b < midY) b = midY;

233	  const pather = (px: number, py: number, destX: number, destY: number): [number, number] => {
234	    { const f = (globalThis as { __swTmPath?: (s: string) => void }).__swTmPath; if (f) f(`${px},${py}->${destX},${destY}`); }
235	    let x = px, y = py;

249	        }
250	      }
251	    }
252	    return [x, y];
253	  };

256	    const r = rooms[n];

258	    for (;;) {

262	      let destX = rng.int(inner.x, inner.x + inner.w - 1);
263	      let destY = rng.int(inner.y, inner.y + inner.h - 1);
264	      if (n === rooms.length - 1) {

284	        } else {

288	      } else {

516	  //      每轮随机选一间 → 房内随机点（须墙 87 且空），Next(2) 定竖/横走线方向,
517	  //      沿该向找首个实心后回退一格,以落点为心 ±(3..9) 方框把【贴走线面】的实心格
518	  //      转 232（SolidTile 且反向邻格非实心才转;排除框内见门/祭坛）;单轮有放置
519	  //      才扣预算,连续 1000 轮无放置也扣预算。★T2:此前整段缺失——全仓 232 只有
520	  //      引用没有放置,神庙木刺为零,本段即其唯一放置源。drunkWorldGen 的三处
521	  //      ±2 加刺分支本仓世界类型不支持,跳过。
522	  {
523	    const SPIKE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_232_wooden_spike']!;
524	    const DOOR_T = TILE_BY_KEY['door_closed']!;
525	    let budget = rooms.length * 1.1;
526	    budget *= 1 + rng.int(-25, 25) * 0.01;                 // cs:34315-34316
527	    let fails = 0;                                         // num113
528	    while (budget > 0) {
529	      fails++;
530	      const room = rooms[rng.int(0, rooms.length - 1)];    // num115 = Next(num2)
531	      let px = rng.int(room.x, room.x + room.w - 1);       // num116
532	      let py = rng.int(room.y, room.y + room.h - 1);       // num117
533	      if (st.inBounds(px, py) && st.wall[st.idx(px, py)] === 87 && !st.flags[st.idx(px, py)]) {
534	        let placed = false;                                // flag6
535	        if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) {
536	          // 竖直走线（cs:34335-34390）：排除框查门 10 **与** 祭坛 237（cs:34349-34358）
537	          const d = rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? 1 : -1;          // num118
538	          while (st.inBounds(px, py) && !st.flags[st.idx(px, py)]) py += d;
539	          py -= d;
540	          let flip = rng.int(0, 1);                        // num119
541	          const rad = rng.int(3, 9);                       // num120 = Next(3,10)
542	          let clear = true;
543	          scan: for (let xx = px - rad; xx < px + rad; xx++) {
544	            for (let yy = py - rad; yy < py + rad; yy++) {
545	              if (!st.inBounds(xx, yy)) continue;
546	              const k = st.idx(xx, yy);
547	              if (st.flags[k] && (st.type[k] === DOOR_T || st.type[k] === ALTAR)) { clear = false; break scan; }
548	            }
549	          }
550	          if (clear) {
551	            for (let xx = px - rad; xx < px + rad; xx++) {
552	              for (let yy = py - rad; yy < py + rad; yy++) {
553	                if (!st.inBounds(xx, yy)) continue;
554	                const k = st.idx(xx, yy);
555	                // 反向邻格实心 = 本格被埋,不转（cs:34366）
556	                if (!solidTileST(st, xx, yy) || st.type[k] === SPIKE || solidTileST(st, xx, yy - d)) continue;
557	                st.type[k] = SPIKE;                        // 原格已实心,仅改 type
558	                placed = true;
559	                const eyy = flip === 0 ? yy - 1 : yy + 1;  // 朝走线面外伸一格(cs:34369-34379)
560	                if (st.inBounds(xx, eyy)) {
561	                  const ke = st.idx(xx, eyy);
562	                  st.type[ke] = SPIKE; st.flags[ke] = 1;
563	                }
564	                flip++; if (flip > 1) flip = 0;
565	              }
566	            }
567	          }
568	          if (placed) { fails = 0; budget -= 1; }
569	        } else {
570	          // 水平走线（cs:34393-34476）：排除框只查门 10,不查祭坛——原版不对称,
571	          // 勿"补全"（cs:34417-34423）
572	          const d = rng.int(0, 1) === 0 ? 1 : -1;          // num125
573	          while (st.inBounds(px, py) && !st.flags[st.idx(px, py)]) px += d;
574	          px -= d;
575	          let flip = rng.int(0, 1);                        // num126
576	          const rad = rng.int(3, 9);                       // num127
577	          let clear = true;
578	          scan: for (let xx = px - rad; xx < px + rad; xx++) {
579	            for (let yy = py - rad; yy < py + rad; yy++) {
580	              if (!st.inBounds(xx, yy)) continue;
581	              const k = st.idx(xx, yy);
582	              if (st.flags[k] && st.type[k] === DOOR_T) { clear = false; break scan; }
583	            }
584	          }
585	          if (clear) {
586	            for (let xx = px - rad; xx < px + rad; xx++) {
587	              for (let yy = py - rad; yy < py + rad; yy++) {
588	                if (!st.inBounds(xx, yy)) continue;
589	                const k = st.idx(xx, yy);
590	                if (!solidTileST(st, xx, yy) || st.type[k] === SPIKE || solidTileST(st, xx - d, yy)) continue;
591	                st.type[k] = SPIKE;
592	                placed = true;
593	                const exx = flip === 0 ? xx - 1 : xx + 1;
594	                if (st.inBounds(exx, yy)) {
595	                  const ke = st.idx(exx, yy);
596	                  st.type[ke] = SPIKE; st.flags[ke] = 1;
597	                }
598	                flip++; if (flip > 1) flip = 0;
599	              }
600	            }
601	          }
602	          if (placed) { fails = 0; budget -= 1; }
603	        }
604	      }
605	      if (fails > 1000) { fails = 0; budget -= 1; }        // cs:34480-34484
606	    }
607	  }
608	  tmMark('spikes');
609	  // ---- makeTemple 尾（cs:34487-34491）：房间 bbox 四侧各外扩 10 → GenVars ----
610	  // 原版在函数尾写 GenVars.tLeft/tRight/tTop/tBottom/tRooms 供**独立注册**的
611	  // LihzahrdTemplePart2 pass（cs:17809）消费；早退路径不写（字段保持初值 0，
612	  // Part2 预算 0×掷骰照掷=原版语义）。2026-08-16 拆分前 templePart2 内联于此。
613	  {
614	    let tL = Infinity, tR = -Infinity, tT = Infinity, tB = -Infinity;
615	    for (const r of rooms) {
616	      tL = Math.min(tL, r.x); tR = Math.max(tR, r.x + r.w);
617	      tT = Math.min(tT, r.y); tB = Math.max(tB, r.y + r.h);
618	    }
619	    gs.tLeft = Math.floor(tL) - 10;
620	    gs.tRight = Math.ceil(tR) + 10;
621	    gs.tTop = Math.floor(tT) - 10;
622	    gs.tBottom = Math.ceil(tB) + 10;
623	    gs.tRooms = rooms.length;
624	  }
625	  // 注：LihzahrdAltar 终保 pass（cs:21753-21791）原版是 TileCleanup(21327) 之后、
626	  // MicroBiomes(21785) 之前的独立 pass（Pots/Traps/TileCleanup 可能扰动祭坛后再
627	  // 重放）。2026-08-13 前并入本函数末尾（执行位≈15911），已外提为
628	  // runLihzahrdAltarPass 独立槽位（WorldGen.ts「蜥蜴祭坛」槽）。
629	}
630	
631	/** LihzahrdTemplePart2 pass（cs:17809-17817 注册体 1:1）：神庙陈设五段独立槽
632	 *  （Moss(17573) 之后、CaveWalls(17819) 之前——WorldGen.ts「神庙陈设」槽）。
633	 *  ★pass 头翻转（cs:17813-17814 `tileSolid[162]=false; tileSolid[226]=true`：
634	 *  162 薄冰非实/226 蜥蜴砖实）；尾 cs:17816 `tileSolid[232]=false`（木刺非实心，
635	 *  CaveWalls 头 cs:17823 重放、BeeLarva 尾 cs:21048 还原 true）。2026-08-16 拆分前
636	 *  内联于 runTemplePass（执行位≈15911，比原版 Part2 注册位早约 1900 行）。 */
637	export function runTemplePart2Pass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
638	  flipGenSolid([162], false);
639	  flipGenSolid([226], true);
640	  templePart2(st, rng, gs, chests);
641	  flipGenSolid([232], false);
642	}
643	
644	/** LihzahrdAltar 终保 pass（cs:21753-21791 1:1）：重放祭坛 6 格 + 正下方三格
645	 *  强制 226 地板（slope/halfBrick/liquid 复位）——原版注册序在 TileCleanup(21327)
646	 *  之后，对中途 Pots/Traps/TileCleanup 的祭坛扰动做最终回滚（顺带达成
647	 *  cs:21744-21746 的 232→226；TileCleanupPass ⑪ 段同效双保险）。 */
648	export function runLihzahrdAltarPass(st: TileStore, gs: GenState): void {
649	  const altarX = gs.lAltarX, altarY = gs.lAltarY;
650	  if (altarX < 0) return;   // 无神庙世界（Skyblock 等）跳过
651	  for (let dx = 0; dx <= 2; dx++) {
652	    for (let dy = 0; dy <= 1; dy++) {
653	      if (st.inBounds(altarX + dx, altarY + dy)) {
654	        st.setTileSilent(altarX + dx, altarY + dy, ALTAR, dx * 18, dy * 18);
655	      }
656	    }
657	    const bj = st.inBounds(altarX + dx, altarY + 2) ? st.idx(altarX + dx, altarY + 2) : -1;
658	    if (bj >= 0) {
659	      st.type[bj] = BRICK; st.flags[bj] = 1;
660	      st.slope[bj] = 0; st.half[bj] = 0; st.liquid[bj] = 0;
661	    }
662	  }
663	}
664	
665	/** templePart2（1456 cs:34497-34685）1:1。tRooms=房间数；各段预算公式与
666	 *  失败推进语义逐掷对齐（陷阱段 fails>100 直接扣预算；其余段 fails>10000 break）。
667	 *  采样域读 GenVars（runTemplePass 尾写入的**外扩后** bbox——cs:34487-34491，
668	 *  T5：外扩 10 是写入侧语义；rx/by 的 -1 是 Next 上界排他换算）。 */
669	function templePart2(
670	  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState,
671	  chests: ChestSink,
672	): void {
673	  const lx = gs.tLeft, rx = gs.tRight - 1;
674	  const ty = gs.tTop, by = gs.tBottom - 1;
675	  const tRooms = gs.tRooms;
676	
677	  // ---- 1. 尖刺陷阱（cs:34497-34519）：预算 = 房数×1.9×(1±15%) ----
678	  {
679	    let budget = tRooms * 1.9 * (1 + rng.int(-15, 15) * 0.01);
680	    let fails = 0;
681	    while (budget > 0) {
682	      const x2 = rng.int(lx, rx);
683	      const y2 = rng.int(ty, by);
684	      if (st.inBounds(x2, y2) && st.wall[st.idx(x2, y2)] === 87 && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, y2)]) {
685	        if (mayanTrap(st, rng, gs, x2, y2)) { budget -= 1; fails = 0; }
686	        else fails++;
687	      } else fails++;
688	      if (fails > 100) { fails = 0; budget -= 1; }   // cs:34517-34518
689	    }
690	  }
691	  // ★cs:34530 `Main.tileSolid[232] = false`（尖刺陷阱段后、宝箱段前）：木刺非实心,
692	  //   后续箱/雕像/家具/挂饰段的 SolidTile 锚定不再踩在 232 上。函数尾 cs:34701
693	  //   还原 true（见函数尾）——净效果为零,纯段内作用域。
694	  flipGenSolid([232], false);
695	
696	  // ---- 2. 神庙宝箱（cs:34521-34545）：预算 = 房数×0.35×(1±15%) ----
697	  //  AddBuriedChest(mainItem=1293 蜥蜴能量电池, notNearOtherChests:true, style 16)
698	  {
699	    let budget = tRooms * 0.35 * (1 + rng.int(-15, 15) * 0.01);
700	    let fails = 0;
701	    while (budget > 0) {
702	      const x2 = rng.int(lx, rx);
703	      const y2 = rng.int(ty, by);
704	      // ★T6:已删除自制"祭坛±3 回避"前置门——原版 cs:34531-34538 无此门,真正排除
705	      //   在 AddBuriedChest 内部（cs:35992-35999,以落地行 k 为锚 5×5 查
706	      //   Boulders/26/237 拒）;本仓 placeBuriedChest 已带等价检查。
707	      if (st.inBounds(x2, y2) && st.wall[st.idx(x2, y2)] === 87 && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, y2)]) {
708	        // notNearOtherChests:true(cs:34532 原版实参)
709	        if (placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x2, y2, { style: 16, loot: 'vi_1293_lihzahrd_power_cell', notNearOtherChests: true })) {
710	          budget -= 1;
711	          fails = 0;
712	        }
713	      }
714	      fails++;
715	      if (fails > 10000) break;
716	    }
717	  }
718	
719	  // ---- 3. 雕像 105 style43-45（cs:34547-34583）：预算 = 房数×1.25×(1+Next(-25,36)%) ----
720	  {
721	    let budget = tRooms * 1.25 * (1 + rng.int(-25, 35) * 0.01);
722	    let fails = 0;
723	    while (budget > 0) {
724	      fails++;
725	      const x2 = rng.int(lx, rx);
726	      const y2 = rng.int(ty, by);
727	      if (st.inBounds(x2, y2) && st.wall[st.idx(x2, y2)] === 87 && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, y2)]) {
728	        let fy = y2;
729	        while (st.inBounds(x2, fy) && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, fy)]) {
730	          fy++;
731	          if (fy > by) break;
732	        }
733	        fy--;
734	        if (fy <= by) {
735	          const style = rng.int(43, 45);              // Next(43,46)
736	          if (placeStatueTemple(st, x2, fy, style)) budget -= 1;
737	        }
738	      }
739	      if (fails > 10000) break;
740	    }
741	  }
742	
743	  // ---- 4. 蜥蜴家具（cs:34585-34632）：预算 = 房数×1.35×(1+Next(-15,26)%) ----
744	  //  Next(3)（cs:34608-34630 原文配对，勿旋转）：0→工作台 18 style10 /
745	  //  1→桌 14 style9 / 2→椅 15 style12（曾把三对整体错抄一位→神庙出木椅）
746	  {
747	    let budget = tRooms * 1.35 * (1 + rng.int(-15, 25) * 0.01);
748	    let fails = 0;
749	    while (budget > 0) {
750	      fails++;
751	      const x2 = rng.int(lx, rx);
752	      const y2 = rng.int(ty, by);
753	      if (st.inBounds(x2, y2) && st.wall[st.idx(x2, y2)] === 87 && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, y2)]) {
754	        let fy = y2;
755	        while (st.inBounds(x2, fy) && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, fy)]) {
756	          fy++;
757	          if (fy > by) break;
758	        }
759	        fy--;
760	        if (fy <= by) {
761	          const kind = rng.int(0, 2);                 // Next(3)
762	          const spec = kind === 0
763	            ? { tile: TILE_BY_KEY['workbench']!, style: 10 }
764	            : kind === 1
765	              ? { tile: TILE_BY_KEY['table']!, style: 9 }
766	              : { tile: TILE_BY_KEY['chair']!, style: 12 };
767	          if (placeFurn(st, x2, fy, spec.tile, spec.style)) budget -= 1;
768	        }
769	      }
770	      if (fails > 10000) break;
771	    }
772	  }
773	
774	  // ---- 5. 墙挂饰 240 style88（cs:34634-34682）：数量 = 1 + (w>4200) + (w>6400?Next(2):0) ----
775	  {
776	    let count = 1;
777	    if (st.w > 4200) count++;
778	    if (st.w > 6400) count += rng.int(0, 1);
779	    let fails = 0;
780	    while (count > 0) {
781	      fails++;
782	      const x2 = rng.int(lx, rx);
783	      const y2 = rng.int(ty, by);
784	      if (st.inBounds(x2, y2) && st.wall[st.idx(x2, y2)] === 87 && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, y2)]) {
785	        // ±70 内已有 240，或 ±4 内有蜥蜴砖 226 → 拒绝（cs:34654-34675）
786	        let flag = false;
787	        for (let dx = -70; dx <= 70 && !flag; dx++) {
788	          for (let dy = -70; dy <= 70; dy++) {
789	            const xx = x2 + dx, yy = y2 + dy;
790	            if (!st.inBounds(xx, yy)) continue;
791	            const k = st.idx(xx, yy);
792	            if (!st.flags[k]) continue;
793	            const sh = TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
794	            if (sh === 240) { flag = true; break; }
795	            if (dx >= -4 && dx <= 4 && dy >= -4 && dy <= 4 && sh === 226) { flag = true; break; }
796	          }
797	        }
798	        if (!flag && placeWallHangingTemple(st, x2, y2, 88)) count--;
799	      }
800	      if (fails > 10000) break;
801	    }
802	  }
803	  // ★cs:34701 `Main.tileSolid[232] = true`：函数尾还原（对应段内 cs:34530 翻转）
804	  flipGenSolid([232], true);
805	}
806	
807	/** PlaceTile(105, style)（神庙雕像）：2×3，锚点=底行左列的下一格语义（与 StatuesPass 一致） */
808	function placeStatueTemple(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, style: number): boolean {
809	  const STATUE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_105_statue'];
810	  if (STATUE === undefined) return false;
811	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
812	    for (let dy = -2; dy <= 0; dy++) {
813	      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy) || st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)]) return false;
814	    }
815	  }
816	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
817	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) {
818	      // 雕像帧 55 列换行（cs:39716-39720；神庙 style 43-45 <55 本可不过防御性同款）
819	      const row = Math.floor(style / 55);
820	      st.setTileSilent(x + dx, y - 2 + dy, STATUE, (style - row * 55) * 36 + dx * 18, row * 54 + dy * 18);
821	    }
822	  }
823	  return true;
824	}
825	
826	/** PlaceTile(240, style)（神庙墙挂饰）：3×3，帧基 = (style%36)*54 / floor(style/36)*54 */
827	function placeWallHangingTemple(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, style: number): boolean {
828	  const HANGING = TILE_BY_KEY['v_240_wall_hangings_3x3'];
829	  if (HANGING === undefined) return false;
830	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {
831	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) {
832	      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy) || st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)]) return false;
833	    }
834	  }
835	  const bx = (style % 36) * 54;
836	  const bys = Math.floor(style / 36) * 54;
837	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {
838	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) {
839	      st.setTileSilent(x + dx, y + dy, HANGING, bx + dx * 18, bys + dy * 18);
840	    }
841	  }
842	  return true;
843	}
844	
845	/** 玛雅陷阱(WorldGen.cs:2945-3235):压板 135(style6)→ 陷阱 137,路径铺三色随机导线 */
846	function mayanTrap(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, x2: number, y2: number): boolean {
847	  const PLATE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_135_pressure_plates']!;
848	  const TRAP = TILE_BY_KEY['v_137_traps']!;
849	  const solid = (x: number, y: number) => st.inBounds(x, y) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] !== 0;
850	  const wireBit = (n: number) => n === 0 ? TileStore.WIRE_RED : n === 1 ? TileStore.WIRE_BLUE : TileStore.WIRE_GREEN;
851	  const setW = (x: number, y: number, n: number) => { st.wire[st.idx(x, y)] |= wireBit(n); };
852	  const getW = (x: number, y: number) => {
853	    const b = st.wire[st.idx(x, y)];
854	    if (b & TileStore.WIRE_RED) return 0;
855	    if (b & TileStore.WIRE_BLUE) return 1;
856	    if (b & TileStore.WIRE_GREEN) return 2;
857	    return -1;
858	  };
859	  const pickColor = (x: number, y: number) => {
860	    const ex = getW(x, y);
861	    return ex >= 0 ? ex : rng.int(0, 2);
862	  };
863	  // num1:1/3 概率横向(L2947-2949)
864	  const kind = rng.int(0, 2) === 0 ? 0 : 1;
865	  // 落点向下找实心(L2950-2962)
866	  const px = x2;
867	  let fy = y2;
868	  while (!solid(px, fy)) {
869	    fy++;
870	    if (fy >= st.h - 300) return false;
871	  }
872	  const sh = TILE_BY_KEY['v_226_lihzahrd_brick']!;
873	  if (st.type[st.idx(px, fy)] === sh || TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(px, fy)]]?.vanilla?.sheet === 10) return false;
874	  const j = fy - 1;
875	  if (st.liquid[st.idx(px, j)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(px, j)] === 2) return false;
876	  // 周围须净空(L2967)

73	function diff(st: TileStore, idx: number, label: string, maxPairs = 12) {

86	    if (!(dT || dA || dW || dL)) continue;

88	    if (dL) liq++;
89	    const x = i % W, y = (i / W) | 0;

92	  }
93	  console.log(`${label}: typ=${typ} act=${act} wal=${wal} liq=${liq}`);

95	  console.log('  regions:', JSON.stringify([...regs.entries()].sort((p, q) => q[1] - p[1]).slice(0, 6)));
96	  console.log('  samples:', samples.join(' | '));
97	  return { typ, act, wal, liq };
98	}
99	
100	function countedRng(seed: number) {
101	  const rng = new RNG(seed);
102	  const ur = (rng as unknown as { ur: { internalSample: () => number } }).ur;
103	  const orig = ur.internalSample.bind(ur);
104	  const box = { n: 0 };
105	  ur.internalSample = () => { box.n++; return orig(); };
106	  return { rng, box };
107	}
108	
109	function streamHashOf(ur: UnifiedRandom): string {
110	  const sac = (ur as unknown as { seedArray: Int32Array; inext: number });
111	  let h = 0x811c9dc5;
112	  const step = (b: number) => { h = Math.imul(h ^ (b & 0xff), 16777619) >>> 0; };
113	  for (let i = 0; i < sac.seedArray.length; i++) {
114	    const v = sac.seedArray[i] | 0;
115	    for (let b = 0; b < 4; b++) step(v >>> (8 * b));
116	  }
117	  const ix = sac.inext >>> 0;
118	  for (let b = 0; b < 4; b++) step(ix >>> (8 * b));
119	  return h.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
120	}
121	
122	/** 暴力定标：fresh(seed) 消耗 N 次后 StreamHash == want（上限 hi） */
123	function calibrateN(seed: number, want: string, hi = 3_000_000): number {
124	  const ur = new UnifiedRandom(seed);
125	  for (let n = 0; n <= hi; n++) {
126	    if (streamHashOf(ur) === want) return n;
127	    (ur as unknown as { internalSample: () => number }).internalSample();
128	  }
129	  return -1;
130	}

137	    else if (v instanceof Int32Array) c[k] = v.slice();
138	    else if (v && typeof v === 'object') c[k] = { ...(v as Record<string, unknown>) };
139	  }
140	  return c as unknown as GenState;
141	}

function setBrick(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {

// 种子等价 diff（L2 工具）：我们 generateWorld(同种子+同 evil) 的网格
// ↔ 原版 1.4.5.6 金标 .wld 逐格对比。
// 当前处于 L1 迭代期：本测试只断言工具链自洽（维度一致），

// 跑全 5 金标：SEED_PARITY_ALL=1 npx vitest run tests/seed-parity.test.ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';
import { GOLDEN_WORLDS, loadGolden } from './golden-wld-meta.test';

/** 内部 tile id → 原版 tile id（TILE_DEFS[].vanilla.sheet 即原版 tile id） */
const V_ID = new Int16Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);
for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
  const sheet = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
  if (typeof sheet === 'number') V_ID[i] = sheet;
}

interface DiffReport {
  cells: number;
  typeMismatch: number;
  wallMismatch: number;
  liquidMismatch: number;
  topTypePairs: Array<[string, number]>;
  byXBand: Array<{ band: number; mismatch: number }>;
  byYBand: Array<{ band: string; mismatch: number }>;
  surface: { exactCols: number; meanAbsDy: number; firstDx: number; corr: number };
  sample: Array<{ x: number; y: number; ours: string; golden: string }>;
}

function diffSeed(seedText: string, evil: 0 | 1, file: string): DiffReport {
  const g = loadGolden(file);
  const gt = g.tiles;
  return generateWorld({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText, evil }).then((world) => {
    const st = world.store;
    const n = gt.type.length;
    let typeMismatch = 0, wallMismatch = 0, liquidMismatch = 0;
    const pairs = new Map<string, number>();
    const xBand = new Float64Array(21); // 每 200 列一段
    const yBands = ['天空0-90', '表层90-300', '洞穴300-600', '深层600-900', '地狱900+'].map((band) => ({ band, mismatch: 0 }));
    const sample: DiffReport['sample'] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      // --- tile type ---
      const ourType = st.flags[i] ? V_ID[st.type[i]] ?? -2 : 0;
      const goldenType = gt.active[i] ? gt.type[i] : 0;
      const y = (i / 4200) | 0;
      const x = i % 4200;
      let mismatch = false;
      if (ourType !== goldenType) {
        typeMismatch++;
        mismatch = true;
        const key = `${ourType < 0 ? `OURS(${st.type[i]})` : ourType}→${goldenType}`;
        pairs.set(key, (pairs.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);
        if (sample.length < 8) sample.push({ x, y, ours: key.split('→')[0], golden: String(goldenType) });
      }
      // --- wall ---
      if (st.wall[i] !== gt.wall[i]) { wallMismatch++; mismatch = true; }
      // --- liquid（类型+有无；液量有沉降时序差异，先比类型格数） ---
      const ourL = st.liquid[i] > 0 ? st.liquidType[i] : 0;
      const goldenL = gt.liquid[i] > 0 ? gt.liquidType[i] : 0;
      if (ourL !== goldenL) { liquidMismatch++; mismatch = true; }

  }
  return false;

/** SolidOrSlopedTile（cs:70064）：active && tileSolid && !tileSolidTop && !inActive。
 *  slope/halfBrick 不参与（半砖/坡面仍是"实或坡"）。生成期无 actuator/平台类活性块，
 *  tileSolidTop 恒 false（本 pass 阶段世界无平台/桌子类），inActive 恒 false。 */
function solidOrSlopedAt(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  return st.inBounds(x, y) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] !== 0
    && genSolidType(st.type[st.idx(x, y)]);
}

function solidTileAt(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  // ★生成期实心走 genSolidType（cs:12537 起生成期 tileSolid[484]=false）：本 pass
  // 在 Desert 之后，CA 初值/放置门/清理门的 SolidTile 查询若用裸 TILE_DEFS.solid
  // （484=true），与 oracle 的 SOLID.Remove(484) 分叉——地下沙漠边缘的 484 会被
  // JS 当实心花岗岩腔壁（granite checkpoint 三哈希全差）。
  // ★SolidTile（cs:70155）还要求 !halfBrick && slope==0——PlaceSlab 每格调
  //  Tile.SmoothSlope 给大理石格上坡面/半砖后，后续 PlaceTight 门的 SolidTile 查询
  //  对这些格必须判非实心（否则门骰多掷 → pass 流全体错位）
  if (!solidOrSlopedAt(st, x, y)) return false;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  return st.half[i] === 0 && st.slope[i] === 0;
}

/** CanPoundTile（cs:81434）排除表（switch default true）：SmoothSlope 前置门 */
const NO_POUND_SHEETS = new Set([10, 48, 137, 232, 380, 387, 388, 476, 484]);
const sheetOfTile = (st: TileStore, i: number): number =>
  TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;

/** Tile.SmoothSlope（Tile.cs:822，applyToNeighbors=true）1:1。
 *  邻序 (x+1,y)→(x-1,y)→(x,y+1)→(x,y-1) 后自身；CanPoundTile||SolidOrSlopedTile 门；
 *  四邻 mask（上"非空"bit3 用 TileEmpty 语义）→ 半砖/坡面重分类。无掷骰但改
 *  half/slope 状态 → 影响后续 SolidTile 门与最终坡面。 */
function tileSmoothSlope(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {
  for (const [nx, ny] of [[x + 1, y], [x - 1, y], [x, y + 1], [x, y - 1]] as Array<[number, number]>) {
    smoothSlopeSelf(st, nx, ny);
  }
  smoothSlopeSelf(st, x, y);
}

function smoothSlopeSelf(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  if (NO_POUND_SHEETS.has(sheetOfTile(st, i)) || !solidOrSlopedAt(st, x, y)) return;
  // TileEmpty(x,y-1)（cs:70037）：非 active（或 actuator 失活）即空
  const aboveNotEmpty = st.inBounds(x, y - 1) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y - 1)] !== 0;
  const aboveNotSolid = !solidOrSlopedAt(st, x, y - 1);
  const mask = ((aboveNotEmpty ? 1 : 0) << 3)
    | ((solidOrSlopedAt(st, x, y + 1) ? 1 : 0) << 2)
    | ((solidOrSlopedAt(st, x - 1, y) ? 1 : 0) << 1)
    | (solidOrSlopedAt(st, x + 1, y) ? 1 : 0);
  const flag2 = aboveNotSolid && aboveNotEmpty;
  switch (mask) {
    case 10: if (!flag2) { st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 3; } break;
    case 9: if (!flag2) { st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 4; } break;
    case 6: st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 1; break;
    case 5: st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 2; break;
    case 4: st.slope[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 1; break;
    default: st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 0; break;
  }
}

/** TileID.Sets.Ore（TileID.cs:399，19 个）——大理石/花岗岩 ResetToType 的矿石保留判定。
 *  曾抄成 20/203/70/21/116/234 的杂表 → 花岗岩体内矿石被吞（granite checkpoint 纯 type 差） */
const VANILLA_ORE = new Set([7, 166, 6, 167, 9, 168, 8, 169, 22, 204, 37, 58, 107, 221, 108, 222, 111, 223, 211]);
function isOreType(t: number): boolean {
  const v = TILE_DEFS[t]?.vanilla?.sheet;
  return v != null && VANILLA_ORE.has(v);
}

export function runMarbleGranitePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  // Marble(12800)/Granite(12884) 各自是独立 GenPass——RunPass 每 pass 前重播种子
  rng.reseed(gs.seed); // ▶Marble cs:12800
  runMarblePass(st, rng, gs);
  rng.reseed(gs.seed); // ▶Granite cs:12884

export function runFloatingIslandsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  const { w, h } = st;
  const isCount = Math.trunc(w * 0.0008);          // 3（4200 宽）
  const skyLakes = 1;                               // GenVars.skyLakes（w>8000/6000 才 +1/+1）
  const total = isCount + skyLakes;
  const housesX: number[] = [];
  const housesY: number[] = [];
  let lakes = 0;
  for (let n = 0; n < total; n++) {
    let budget = w;
    while (--budget > 0) {
      let ok = true;
      let x = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.1), Math.floor(w * 0.9) - 1);   // Next(0.1w, 0.9w)
      while (x > w / 2 - 150 && x < w / 2 + 150) x = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.1), Math.floor(w * 0.9) - 1);
      for (let j = 0; j < housesX.length; j++) {
        if (x > housesX[j]! - 180 && x < housesX[j]! + 180) { ok = false; break; }
      }
      if (!ok) continue;
      ok = false;
      let surf = 0;
      for (let y = 200; y < gs.worldSurface && y < h; y++) {
        if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) { surf = y; ok = true; break; }
      }
      if (!ok) continue;
      budget = -1;
      // GenVars.worldSurfaceLow（raw 截断）＝ gs.worldSurfaceLow（正数 floor≡trunc）
      const y = Math.min(rng.int(90, surf - 101), gs.worldSurfaceLow - 50);
      // GenVars.worldSurfaceLow（raw double 截断）——注意非 gs.worldSurfaceLow
      const isLake = lakes >= isCount;
          if (isLake) cloudLake(st, rng, x, y);
      else cloudIsland(st, rng, x, y);
          housesX.push(x); housesY.push(y);
      gs.floatingIslands.push({ x, y, style: 0, isLake });
      if (!isLake) lakes++;
    }
  }
}

/** WillWaterPlacedHereStayPut（cs:79050）：下/左/右三邻 = (活性实心非平台) 或 满水 */
function waterStaysPut(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  const side = (ax: number, ay: number): boolean => {
    if (!st.inBounds(ax, ay)) return false;
    const k = st.idx(ax, ay);
    return (st.flags[k] !== 0 && !!TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]]?.solid && !TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]]?.platform)
      || st.liquid[k] === 255;
  };
  return side(x, y + 1) && side(x - 1, y) && side(x + 1, y);
}

/** CloudIsland（cs:78618-79066）1:1：①云盘(189) → ②底缘雨云凸包 → ③土芯(云→土) →
 *  ④土面云斑 → ⑤墙 73 → ⑥岛面水池 → ⑦岛上方云 puff + 中心注水 */
function cloudIsland(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, ci: number, cj: number): void {
  const tile = CLOUD;                                // num8 = 189（雨云只在凸包/puff 出现）
  let num = rng.int(100, 149);                       // Next(100,150)
  let num2 = num;
  let num3 = rng.int(20, 29);                        // Next(20,30)
  let b4 = ci, b5 = ci, b6 = cj, b7 = cj;            // bbox
  let px = ci + 0.0, py = cj + 0.0;
  let vx = rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.2;
  while (vx > -2 && vx < 2) vx = rng.int(-20, 20) * 0.2;
  let vy = rng.int(-20, -11) * 0.02;

'四通道同' : '★四通道异'}`;\n      prov.set(key, (prov.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);\n      if ((cellsByProv.get(key) ?? []).length < 6) {\n        const arr = cellsByProv.get(key) ?? [];\n        arr.push(`(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0})`);\n        cellsByProv.set(key, arr);\n      }\n    }\n    else if (!js && tr) miss++;\n    else diffVal++;\n  }\n  console.log(`全图@52: 幻影=${phantom} 漏=${miss} 值差=${diffVal}`);\n  for (const [k, v] of [...prov.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])) {\n    console.log(`  ${String(v).padStart(4)}  ${k}   ${(cellsByProv.get(k) ?? []).join(' ')}`);\n  }"
}

  const bn7 = Math.trunc(bn / 2), bn8 = Math.trunc(bn2 / 2);
  const bn9 = (bn8 + 1) * (bn8 + 1);
  const vv = rng.next() * 2 - 1, vv2 = rng.next() * 2 - 1, vv3 = rng.next() * 2 - 1;
  let n11 = 0;
  for (let m = 0; m <= bn; m++) {
    const n12 = bn8 / bn7 * (m - bn7);
    const n13 = Math.min(bn8, Math.trunc(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, bn9 - n12 * n12))));
    n11 = m >= Math.trunc(bn / 2)
      ? n11 + (vv2 + (vv3 - vv2) * (m / Math.trunc(bn / 2) - 1))
      : n11 + (vv + (vv2 - vv) * (m / Math.trunc(bn / 2)));
    for (let n = bn8 - n13; n <= bn8 + n13; n++) {
      // PlaceSlab（scale=3）
      const pOX = m * 3 + ox, pOY = n * 3 + oy + Math.trunc(n11);
      const pst = state[m + 1]![n + 1]!, pWall = hasWall[m + 1]![n + 1]!;
      const __probe = (globalThis as { __swSlabProbe?: (x: number, y: number, pst: number, pWall: boolean) => void }).__swSlabProbe;
      if (__probe) __probe(pOX, pOY, pst, pWall);
      let pn4 = 0, pn5 = 3;
      for (let i = -1; i < 4; i++) {
        if ((i === -1 || i === 3) && rng.int(0, 1) === 0) continue;   // 边缘列 50% 跳过
        if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) pn4--;
        if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) pn5++;
        for (let j = pn4; j < pn5; j++) {
          const px = pOX + i, py = pOY + j;
          if (!st.inBounds(px, py)) continue;
          const ti = st.idx(px, py);
          st.type[ti] = isOreType(st.type[ti]) ? st.type[ti] : MARBLE;  // ResetToType：矿石保留
          st.flags[ti] = slabSolid(pst, i, j, 3) ? 1 : 0;
          st.liquid[ti] = 0; st.liquidType[ti] = 0;
          st.half[ti] = 0; st.slope[ti] = 0;  // ResetToType 清 sTileHeader（半砖/坡面）
          // ★wall 是 Tile.cs 独立 ushort 字段——ResetToType 的 bTileHeader*=0 不清墙，
          // HasWall=false 时保留旧墙（SquareWallFrame 掷照旧）
          if (pWall) st.wall[ti] = 178;
          // WorldUtils.TileFrame(frameNeighbors:true)（cs:WorldUtils.cs:79-88）：
          // 中心 + (x+1,y)/(x-1,y)/(x,y+1)/(x,y-1) 各一次 TileFrameImportant——
          // 165 格 → CheckStalactite 重查（支撑被后续格上坡 → 击杀/重定型掷）
          frame165(st, rng, px, py);
          frame165(st, rng, px + 1, py);
          frame165(st, rng, px - 1, py);
          frame165(st, rng, px, py + 1);
          frame165(st, rng, px, py - 1);
          // SquareWallFrame：中心格 WallFrame(resetFrame:true) 非零墙掷 1×Next(0,3)
          //（PlaceSlab 每格,cs:MarbleBiome.cs:160-168）
          squareWallFrameRoll(st, rng, px, py);
          // Tile.SmoothSlope（applyToNeighbors=true）：无掷但写 half/slope——
          // 影响本列/邻列后续 SolidTile 门（PlaceTight 骰）与最终坡面
          tileSmoothSlope(st, px, py);
          if (solidTileAt(st, px, py - 1) && rng.int(0, 3) === 0) placeTight(st, rng, px, py);
          if (solidTileAt(st, px, py) && rng.int(0, 3) === 0) placeTight(st, rng, px, py - 1);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return true;
}

/** cs:12884-12975：采样与放置两段分离——先收点（CanPlace = !BiomeTileCheck && !active），
 *  再统一 Place。带宽 (w-200)/num。 */
export function runGranitePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  let count = worldGenRangeCount(st, rng, 4, 8, 'width');
  const pts: Array<[number, number]> = [];
  let placed = 0, fails = 0;
  while (placed < count) {
    const frac = placed / count;
    let x = 0, y = 0, ok = false;
    let budget = 100;
    while (!ok) {
      budget--;
      if (budget <= 0) break;
      const x0 = Math.trunc(frac * (st.w - 200)) + 100;
      x = rng.int(x0, x0 + Math.trunc((st.w - 200) / count) - 1);
      const y0 = Math.trunc(gs.genRockLayer) + 20;
      y = rng.int(y0, y0 + (st.h - (Math.trunc(gs.genRockLayer) + 40) - 200) - 1);
      while (x > st.w * 0.45 && x < st.w * 0.55) x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, st.w - gs.beachDistance - 1);
      ok = true;
    }
    fails++;
    if (ok && !biomeTileCheck(st, x, y) && !(st.inBounds(x, y) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y)])) {
      pts.push([x, y]); placed++;
    } else if (fails > st.w * 10) { count = placed; placed++; fails = 0; }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < count && i < pts.length; i++) granitePlace(st, rng, gs, pts[i]![0]!, pts[i]![1]!);

```

**② _walliso rig 修复**：`gsPristine` 声明在 A 段 it 体内、D 段 temple footprint 独立 it 引用 → ReferenceError。提为模块级（`tools/_walliso.test.ts`），双 it 复验全过（pather 序列全等、rooms=12、226 足迹 bbox 不变）。

## 复验结果

- **@52 神庙簇归零**：typ226 幻影坡清零（残余组中无任何 226 格）；全图幻影 351→**219**（漏=0 值差=0 不变）。
- **[046] 不变**：draws 107,888（-53 木刺段另案照旧）、1,302/1,016/153/0 = L 批终态——重放入口 half/slope 全零，清坡为 no-op，自证不扰动四通道与掷流。
- **[019] 沙漠 bbox** 维持 0/0/0。
- 回归 `{_fullgen-smoke, gen-loot-passes, gen-review-tail, liquid-settle-golden, seed-parity}` 全绿；金标零写；DungeonPass 未动。

## 关键发现（W 批误归因勘正）

残余 219 格**不是神庙债**：溯源 rig（新建 `tools/_x52slope.test.ts`，首写事件+四通道比对，含首写归因回退一窗的口径）定界为 Marble 窗首写 ~160 格 + Granite 窗 ~37 格——其中 82 格纯坡位差（如 (557,797) 双方 typ44 一致但 truth 7×7 全实心 mask=0b1111→slope0，JS 却 s2）、137 格身处花岗岩区四通道债格（js typ483/truth typ44 等）。Marble/Granite 的 PlaceSlab+SmoothSlope 链清坡本就齐全，纯坡位差是花岗岩区 slab 摆放债在平滑掩码上的级联症状——属 d25slab/__swSlabProbe 家族另案（下批标的：先修花岗岩区四通道债，坡位差随之塌缩）。
